# About this item

*    Designed to remove broken studs, bolts, socket screws, and fittings 
*    Aggressive left hand spiral design for extra gripping power 
*    Easy out style spiral flutes embed themselves to increase grip as resistance increases 
*    Extractor size and recommended drill bit size are etched on each tool 
*    "Set includes spiral flute screw extractors sizes EX-1, EX-2, EX-3, EX-4, EX-5; drill bit sizes 5/64", 7/64", 5/32", 1/4", 19/64", and a metal index case" 
*    Included components: Product & Instructions Guide 
*    Power source type: Corded Electric

## From the manufacturer

![Image 30](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)  

![Image 32](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)
For over a century, innovation has been the hallmark of IRWIN TOOLS. Beginning in 1885, with the development of the first IRWIN Auger Bit, and progressing with the introduction of Vise-Grip Locking Tools, Speedbor Flat Bits, Marathon Saw Blades, Quick-Grip Bar Clamps, and Unibit Step Drill Bits, IRWIN has been at the forefront of the industry, creating superior tools for the professional tradesman.

### IRWIN HANSON Screw Extractor and Drill Bit Set, 35 Piece

#### The Serious Solution for Stuck Fasteners

The IRWIN HANSON Bolt-Grip Screw Extractor and Drill Bit Set gives you the extra gripping power to remove problem fasteners without damaging workpieces.

*    Left-hand design for extra gripping power 
*    Sizes etched onto each tool for easy identification 
*    Includes bolt extractors, multi-spline extractors, and drill bits 

![Image 34](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

### Removes the Guesswork

Fasteners vary widely in composition and hardness. That's why this set features extractors specifically designed to make removal easy.

![Image 36](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Easy-Out Style Flutes

Spiral flutes embed themselves deeper as they turn to increase gripping power.

![Image 38](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Multi-Spline Extractors

Designed for high-torque fastener removal applications.

![Image 40](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Tough Drill Bits

Constructed of M-42, 8% cobalt, high speed steel for super abrasive resistance in tough metals.

![Image 42](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

#### Etched Sizes

Fastener and recommended drill bit size etched directly onto each tool to make identification fast and easy.

## Product Description

### Product Description

The IRWIN HANSON Spiral Extractor and Drill Bit Set, 10 Piece, features an aggressive left hand design for extra gripping power. Spiral flutes embed themselves deeper into the metal as the tool is turned so grip increases as resistance increases. With sizes etched onto each tool for quick identification, it includes EX-1, EX-2, EX-3, EX-4, and EX-5 extractors; 5/64", 7/64", 5/32", 1/4", and 19/64" drill bits and a metal index case.

### From the Manufacturer

Irwin Industrial Tools 11119 10-Piece Set Spiral Flute Screw Extractors with Left Hand Cobalt Drill Bits - Metal Index. For harder metals, choose a spiral screw extractor.

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

[Learn more how customers reviews work on Amazon](https://www.amazon.com/gp/help/customer/display.html/ref=cm_cr_dp_d_omni_lm_btn?nodeId=G8UYX7LALQC8V9KA)

### Customers say

Customers find the drill bits and extractors to be of good quality and effective at removing broken bolts, with one customer reporting success with a 14mm broken motor mount. The durability and value for money receive mixed feedback - while some say they can handle tough jobs without breaking, others report bits breaking on first use, and opinions on value are divided between those who find it worth the price and those who don't. The drilling performance and sharpness also get mixed reviews, with some finding them very sharp while others say they dull too soon.

## About this Item

*   Designed to remove broken studs, bolts, socket screws, and fittings
*   Aggressive left hand spiral design for extra gripping power
*   Easy out style spiral flutes embed themselves to increase grip as resistance increases
*   Extractor size and recommended drill bit size are etched on each tool
*   "Set includes spiral flute screw extractors sizes EX-1, EX-2, EX-3, EX-4, EX-5; drill bit sizes 5/64", 7/64", 5/32", 1/4", 19/64", and a metal index case"
*   Included components: Product & Instructions Guide
*   Power source type: Corded Electric

## Product Description

 Product Description 

 The IRWIN HANSON Spiral Extractor and Drill Bit Set, 10 Piece, features an aggressive left hand design for extra gripping power. Spiral flutes embed themselves deeper into the metal as the tool is turned so grip increases as resistance increases. With sizes etched onto each tool for quick identification, it includes EX-1, EX-2, EX-3, EX-4, and EX-5 extractors; 5/64", 7/64", 5/32", 1/4", and 19/64" drill bits and a metal index case. 

 From the Manufacturer 

 Irwin Industrial Tools 11119 10-Piece Set Spiral Flute Screw Extractors with Left Hand Cobalt Drill Bits - Metal Index. For harder metals, choose a spiral screw extractor.
